# 2017 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** look at the phone
**Star:** Face ID · `itt17-faceid` · `sites/iphone/x.html`
**ILS June:** 1,766,926,408 · users BLANK
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 312
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt17-faceid`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2017/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2017/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2017 — 1,766,926,408 · ITU users · bans
2. Face ID / iPhone X — no Home · swipe up
3. Fortnite BR — leftover · free · 100
4. Twitter 280 — type past 140
5. Teams GA — 2016 was preview
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt17-faceid`

**Room:** `years/2017/sites/iphone/x.html`
**Hook:** `data-faceid`
**Nostalgia:** Face ID — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Skip look · Home-button trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt17-faceid`.

**Complete writes**
- Look at the phone / Face ID honesty. Writes itt17-faceid.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `2017` · key `itt17-faceid`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-faceid` — do not write `itt16-*` or `itt18-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Fortnite BR → `years/2017/sites/fortnite/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 312 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt17-airpods17` · sites/airpods17/index.html

**Open:** `/years/2017/sites/airpods17/index.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="airpods17"])`
**Need:** needPick=`airpods17` · field ≥2 (placeholder “airpods17 leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `youtube` · `twitter` · `amazon`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="airpods17"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt17-airpods17']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + wrong pick (not `airpods17`) + Save → “Wrong leftover.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt17-faceid` stays empty. `itt16-airpods17` and `itt18-airpods17` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click `[data-lo-pick="airpods17"]`. Wrong pick never writes.
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “airpods17 leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="airpods17"]`.
- `localStorage['itt17-airpods17']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2017", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt17-airpods17`.
- Does **not** write `itt17-faceid`.
- **Next:** ★ gold → `/years/2017/sites/iphone/x.html`
